## nba.game_lines

Betting lines per game, source, and sportsbook. home_spread is the points added to the home score, so negative means the home team is favored. Moneylines and prices are American odds. kaggle_lines has one line per game from 2007-08, kaggle_books has ten sportsbooks for 2006-07 to 2017-18 with book set, and espn has spreads only from 2012-13.

| `total` | DOUBLE |
| `home_moneyline` | INTEGER |
| `away_moneyline` | INTEGER |

## nba.pbp

NBA Stats play-by-play from 1996-97 with every source column, integer scores, game seconds elapsed, and a garbage_time flag: fourth period or later with a margin before the event of 30, of 25 inside nine minutes, or of 20 inside six. player_id is NULL for team events and also carries coach and referee ids. The assist, block, steal, and fouled player ids from pbp_actors sit on the primary action, never on the separate block and steal rows whose action_type is empty.

## nba.pbp_actors

The second player on a play-by-play action: the assister on a make from 1996-97, the blocker on a miss, the stealer on a turnover, and the fouled player on a foul from 2005-06. ## nba.playoff_series

One row per playoff series from 1946-47, NBA, BAA, and ABA brackets apart, with the league round, format, home court, wins, games in order, each team's Elo entering the series, the Elo chance the home-court team wins it, and next_series_id, the series its winner plays next.
